## Why notes

- 2026-10-24: Jonah Coleman vs Eli Stowers — Oliverwkw: Stowers plays for the eagles
- 2026-10-24: Jaxson Dart vs DJ Moore — BROsenzweig: Dart is MILES too low…
- 2026-10-24: Jaxson Dart vs DJ Moore — Oliverwkw: Dart is the future of the Giants this is a terrible valuation. He's a young top QB I'd take him over anyone.
- 2026-10-24: Michael Pittman Jr. vs Isaac TeSlaa — Oliverwkw: TeSlaa isn't going to do anything
- 2026-10-24: Michael Pittman Jr. vs Isaac TeSlaa — AceMatthew: Pittman had productive seasons, TeSlaa has done nothing and also isn’t even super high potential.
- 2026-10-24: Chuba Hubbard vs Breece Hall — Oliverwkw: Chuba is older and doesn't have the job locked down
- 2026-10-24: Jonah Coleman vs Eli Stowers — Shmuel256: Stowers has been terrible to start the year. Coleman has been gaining value
- 2026-10-24: Chuba Hubbard vs Breece Hall — Shmuel256: Track record
- 2026-10-24: Ray Davis vs Michael Pittman Jr. — Oliverwkw: Davis isn't ever going to do anything
- 2026-10-24: Tyrone Tracy Jr. vs Michael Penix Jr. — Oliverwkw: Penix is BACK he's on the up and up. prob worth a second after a few good weeks
- 2026-10-24: Chuba Hubbard vs Jordan Love — Oliverwkw: Chuba is 27 enjoying his second ever good year. He'll fall soon hes not a long term asset like love
- 2026-10-24: Saquon Barkley vs Antonio Williams — Oliverwkw: Saquon is still great hes not THAT low I don't think
- 2026-10-24: Germie Bernard vs Keon Coleman — Oliverwkw: Germie has a way higher ceiling.
- 2026-10-24: Jaxson Dart vs Germie Bernard — Oliverwkw: The dart ranking is just absurdly low
- 2026-10-24: Christian Watson vs Matthew Stafford — Oliverwkw: Watson feels like he should be worth a tad more. Stafford could retire this year
- 2026-10-24: Josh Allen vs Jaxon Smith-Njigba — Oliverwkw: The gap is too small
- 2026-10-24: Jayden Daniels vs Justin Herbert — Shmuel256: Herbert’s bad year
- 2026-10-24: Ray Davis vs Michael Pittman Jr. — Shmuel256: Davis isnt even rostered
- 2026-10-24: Tyrone Tracy Jr. vs Michael Penix Jr. — Shmuel256: Tracy was a street free agent for most of the year. Penix is on the up and up and still starting
- 2026-10-24: Chris Olave vs Tetairoa McMillan — Shmuel256: Track record. Olave isnt that old either. Tet is worth around the 1.02, only
- 2026-10-24: Carnell Tate vs Brock Purdy — Shmuel256: Not close here. Tate is worth less than tet mcmillan, or at best around the same (good clearly but not yet elite). Purdy is a set and forget qb1 thats worth 2 good firsts at least
- 2026-10-26: DeVonta Smith vs Jaxson Dart — BROsenzweig: Both of these values are WAY too low; each should be at least twice as valuable
- 2026-10-26: TreVeyon Henderson vs Isaiah Likely — Oliverwkw: Likely is TE2 on the year and still 26. Plus he's a giant
- 2026-10-26: Kenyon Sadiq vs Jordan Love — Oliverwkw: Sadiq was 1.8 and he's still worth 1.8
- 2026-10-26: Travis Kelce vs Blake Corum — Oliverwkw: Kelce is on the verge of retirement he's not worth more than a 4th
- 2026-10-26: Marvin Harrison Jr. vs Brenton Strange — Oliverwkw: Neither of these guys is worth more than a high 3, although both have an argument for 2.08 or so
- 2026-10-26: Drake London vs Lamar Jackson — Shmuel256: Im biased here admittedly but jackson’s a top 2 qb when healthy. London isnt a top 5 wr
- 2026-10-26: TreVeyon Henderson vs Isaiah Likely — Shmuel256: This years production difference is really stark. Giving up on hendo personally
- 2026-10-26: Tyler Warren vs Rashee Rice — Shmuel256: Rice is good but injured a lot and apparently in danger of losing his job
- 2026-10-26: Quinshon Judkins vs Garrett Wilson — Shmuel256: Juskins seems average. So nothing exciting. Wilson has top 3 wr upside if they ever get a qb
